Sliding Window 
- Controlled by potentiometer
- left most turn goes to toggle LED
- right most turn goes to image 
- middle pot position goes to uptime ^uLLDJHOr

button
- if button is toggled, can jump to next image
- pot has higher priority, if moved to left. It must move to left ^5ATrlKC9

toggle led
- button press turns led on or off
- Holding the button makes it blink ^jfimhiGH

Feature 2: Shell Command 
- Listens over PC commands with communication via UART
- USB to TTL  ^1l2AWDho

PC ^ituDwUpe

RX ^1ILG2t5K

TX ^y2jwiwbb

USB to TTL ^mMYAvmjQ

STM32F103C6T6 ^rxeWAUDL

RX ^xMwzYqAD

TX ^w4jCnwAR

* **status** - Print system uptime
* **led on** - Turn on onboard LED for 3 seconds
* **pot** - Read potentiometer voltage in mV
* **temp** - Read MCU internal temperature and VREF
* **pwm** - Update and print hardware PWM duty cycle
* **pwm_test** - Run 1000-sample loopback test (PA6 to PA2)
* **pwm_led_dim** - Run 20-second interactive LED dimming via pot
* **i2c_scan** - Scans the I2C bus for any connection and output addr
* **spi_test** - Send bytes 0 to 255 and expect them back unchanged
* **hang** - cause a hang to trigger watchdog
* **ram** - Prints the .data, .bss, free RAM and peak stack use in bytes
* **config** - Adjust the EEPROM memory
* **config_fail** - Simulate a write (name = fail) and cutoff midway, never reaching CRC ^ODd3qhJ0
